  "description": "## Goal\nImplement a new PHP dashboard widget under `ui/widgets/` that allows selecting one or more **supported numeric items** and configuring **shared inclusive** yellow/red thresholds. On refresh, the widget evaluates each selected item’s **latest** numeric value using fixed MVP semantics (**higher_is_worse**, **worst-state-wins**, inclusive boundaries), then renders **one aggregated traffic-light** state.\n\nMVP behavior is fixed:\n- **Aggregation mode:** `worst-state-wins`\n- **Threshold direction:** `higher_is_worse`\n- **Boundary behavior:** inclusive comparisons\n- **Supported items:** numeric items only, specifically **float and unsigned integer**, unless the widget framework enforces a narrower subset (documented explicitly in implementation notes)\n\nPer-item evaluation for `higher_is_worse` (inclusive):\n- `value >= red_threshold` => **red**\n- else `value >= yellow_threshold` => **yellow**\n- else => **green**\n\nAggregation reduction over evaluable items:\n- any red => widget red\n- else any yellow => widget yellow\n- else all green => widget green\n\nThe widget must integrate with the existing dashboard widget framework (add/edit/save/load/permission/refresh/persistence) and must use **standard widget covers** (not custom partial UI) for:\n- invalid configuration\n- empty selection\n- permission loss/inaccessible items\n- unsupported items\n- no-data conditions\n\n## Scope\nIn scope:\n- Add one new widget type in the existing PHP UI widget framework under `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/`.\n- Register it so it appears in the dashboard widget picker and participates in existing add/edit/save/load flows.\n- Provide configuration fields:\n  - widget title/name\n  - selection of one or more items (multiselect)\n  - shared `yellow_threshold`\n  - shared `red_threshold`\n  - `direction` field fixed to MVP `higher_is_worse` (either hidden/locked or validated server-side as always `higher_is_worse` per framework conventions)\n- Persist and restore the configuration using the widget framework’s standard serialization.\n- Server-side validation during render/refresh (and optionally save-time):\n  - threshold parsing as numeric (float)\n  - threshold ordering compatible with `higher_is_worse` (**red_threshold >= yellow_threshold**, inclusive)\n  - at least one item selected\n  - each selected item is present and accessible to current user\n  - each selected item’s type is supported (float + unsigned int; document any narrower enforced subset)\n- During refresh:\n  - retrieve latest values for selected items via existing permission-aware item APIs\n  - evaluate each item (including handling of missing/NULL latest values)\n  - reduce per-item states into one aggregated state using worst-state-wins\n  - return payload for frontend to either show traffic-light or standard cover\n- Deterministic cover precedence for all invalid/exception cases (consistent across save/edit/load/refresh).\n\nOut of scope (for this backlog item):\n- Supporting additional threshold directions or aggregation modes beyond MVP.\n- Client-side custom validation that could diverge from backend validation.\n- Bulk performance optimization beyond using existing latest-value APIs (if available).\n\n## System Changes\n### Backend\n- Create `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` with manifest + PHP entrypoints/classes matching existing widget framework conventions.\n- Define widget config schema + form field definitions using existing shared field/selectors.\n- Implement server-side configuration validation:\n  - parse thresholds; reject non-numeric\n  - enforce `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold` under `higher_is_worse`\n  - enforce non-empty selection\n  - validate each selected item:\n    - existence\n    - permission/accessibility for current user\n    - supported numeric types (float + unsigned int, unless framework requires a narrower subset)\n- Implement refresh/render handler:\n  - fetch latest values for each selected item (permission-aware)\n  - for each item:\n    - if latest value missing/NULL => mark per-item as NO-DATA (drives cover per precedence)\n    - else compute per-item state using inclusive comparisons\n  - reduce states with `worst-state-wins`:\n    - any RED => RED\n    - else any YELLOW => YELLOW\n    - else GREEN\n- Enforce deterministic **widget cover precedence** for all exceptional cases. Implement a single source of truth mapping validation/outcome to cover type.\n  - Example precedence (must be implemented consistently):\n    1) invalid thresholds/config\n    2) empty selection\n    3) unsupported item types present\n    4) permission loss/inaccessible items\n    5) no-data (missing latest values) per defined rule\n    6) valid evaluation => traffic-light\n\n### Frontend\n- Add widget assets under `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/assets/` per manifest conventions.\n- Implement rendering for a **single aggregated traffic-light state** (red/yellow/green) inside the widget container.\n- Ensure when backend indicates a covered state, the frontend shows the standard widget cover UI (no traffic-light should render under cover).\n- Hook into existing widget update lifecycle so the widget updates correctly on refresh.\n\n### Integration points (explicit)\n- Widget picker registration\n- Dashboard widget add/edit UI integration (fields)\n- Widget serialization/deserialization\n- Permission-aware data retrieval\n- Refresh pipeline and response payload contract\n- Standard covers mechanism\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n1. A user can add the widget from the dashboard widget picker, configure **1+ numeric items**, and set **shared inclusive** `yellow_threshold` and `red_threshold`.\n2. The widget direction is fixed/validated to MVP `higher_is_worse`.\n3. On refresh, the widget:\n   - fetches permission-aware latest values for all selected items\n   - computes per-item state using inclusive comparisons\n   - reduces them with worst-state-wins into a single aggregated traffic-light.\n4. For all exceptional situations—invalid thresholds/config, empty selection, unsupported item types, permission loss/inaccessible items, and no-data—the widget shows the **standard widget cover** instead of a traffic-light.\n5. Widget configuration persists across dashboard save/reload and restores correctly.\n6. Access control is enforced: users without access to one or more selected items do not see item values; the widget behaves according to cover precedence.\n\n## Implementation Checklist\n### Backend\n- [ ] Create `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` with manifest + backend entrypoints integrated into widget registry.\n- [ ] Implement config fields: title/name, item multiselect, `yellow_threshold`,   "description": "## Goal\nAdd a new PHP dashboard widget under `ui/widgets/` that lets users select one or more **supported numeric items** and configure shared **inclusive** `yellow_threshold` and `red_threshold` values. On refresh, the widget evaluates each selected item’s **latest numeric value** using fixed MVP semantics (**higher_is_worse**, **worst-state-wins**, inclusive boundaries) and renders **one aggregated traffic-light** state (green/yellow/red).\n\n**MVP semantics (fixed):**\n- Aggregation mode: `worst-state-wins`\n  - any red => widget red\n  - else any yellow => widget yellow\n  - else all green => widget green\n- Threshold direction: `higher_is_worse`\n- Boundary behavior: inclusive comparisons\n- Per-item evaluation (inclusive, higher_is_worse):\n  - `value >= red_threshold` => per-item **red**\n  - else `value >= yellow_threshold` => per-item **yellow**\n  - else => per-item **green**\n\nSupported items (as enforced by implementation notes / widget framework):\n- numeric items only, specifically **float** and **unsigned integer** (or the narrower subset enforced by the widget framework; document explicitly in implementation notes).\n\nWidget must integrate with the existing dashboard widget framework lifecycle (picker + add/edit/save/load + permission-aware render/refresh + persistence) and must use **standard widget covers** (no custom partial UI) for:\n- invalid configuration\n- empty selection\n- permission loss / inaccessible items\n- unsupported items\n- no-data conditions\n\n## Scope\nIn scope:\n1. Create one new widget type in the existing PHP UI widget framework under:\n   - `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/`\n2. Register it so it appears in the dashboard widget picker and participates in all standard widget flows:\n   - add/edit/save/load\n   - permission-aware render/refresh\n   - persistence/serialization\n3. Implement configuration fields:\n   - widget title/name (standard framework field)\n   - multiselect of **one or more items**\n   - shared `yellow_threshold` (numeric/float input)\n   - shared `red_threshold` (numeric/float input)\n   - `direction` is fixed to `higher_is_worse` (either hidden or validated server-side as invariant per framework conventions)\n4. Implement server-side validation during render/refresh (and optionally save-time if the framework supports it):\n   - threshold parsing as numeric (float)\n   - enforce ordering compatible with `higher_is_worse`: **`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`** (inclusive)\n   - require at least one item selected\n   - each selected item must exist and be accessible to the current user\n   - each selected item’s type must be supported (float and unsigned integer per MVP; align to any narrower subset enforced by the framework and document explicitly)\n5. Implement refresh evaluation:\n   - resolve each configured item to a latest numeric value (per item)\n   - if latest numeric value is missing/unavailable => standard **no-data** cover\n   - compute per-item state using inclusive MVP rules\n   - aggregate using worst-state-wins\n   - return a single aggregated state payload suitable for standard widget rendering\n6. Ensure widget output uses standard covers for failure modes rather than custom partial UI.\n\nOut of scope:\n- Changing dashboard widget framework semantics\n- Adding non-numeric item support\n- Non-MVP threshold directions\n- Custom client-side visualization beyond what other traffic-light widgets already use\n\n## System Changes\n### Backend\n- Add new widget implementation under `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` including:\n  - widget manifest/registration file(s) consistent with the repo\n  - PHP widget class(es) consistent with existing widgets’ structure\n  - configuration schema/field wiring for title, multiselect items, and thresholds\n- Implement persistence/serialization:\n  - store selected itemids and threshold values in the framework’s standard serialization format\n  - restore them on widget load\n- Implement refresh-time validation and cover mapping:\n  - invalid config (non-numeric thresholds, threshold ordering violation, direction invariant breach if applicable) => standard INVALID_CONFIG cover\n  - empty selection => standard EMPTY/NO_ITEMS cover\n  - inaccessible/permission-lost items => standard PERMISSION/INACCESSIBLE cover\n  - unsupported item types => standard UNSUPPORTED_ITEMS cover\n  - no latest values available => standard NO_DATA cover\n- Implement item resolution and supported-type filtering:\n  - resolve configured itemids into items accessible to the current user\n  - verify supported numeric types (float and unsigned integer, or narrower subset enforced by framework)\n- Implement latest numeric value retrieval:\n  - fetch latest available numeric value per selected item\n  - define/align behavior for null/NaN/missing values to trigger the standard no-data cover\n- Implement MVP evaluation:\n  - per-item state: `>= red_threshold` => red; else `>= yellow_threshold` => yellow; else green\n  - aggregate: any red => red; else any yellow => yellow; else green\n- Provide reason/debug metadata for UI rendering (if the framework supports it) without leaking inaccessible data.\n\n### Frontend\n- Use the widget framework’s standard widget rendering mechanisms and standard widget covers.\n- Render a single aggregated traffic-light UI/state (one of green/yellow/red) based on the refresh payload.\n- Add any minimal JS/assets only if required by the framework; ] Items multiselect\n  - [ ] `yellow_threshold` (float parsing)\n  - [ ] `red_threshold` (float parsing)\n  - [ ] `direction` fixed/locked to `higher_is_worse` (hidden or server-validated invariant)\n- [ ] Implement persistence/serialization hooks for the framework.\n- [ ] Implement refresh-time validation and cover selection:\n  - [ ] at least one item selected\n  - [ ] thresholds numeric parsing\n  - [ ] `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`\n  - [ ] all items exist and are accessible\n  - [ ] all items supported numeric types (float + unsigned int or documented subset)\n- [ ] Implement latest numeric value retrieval for each configured item.\n- [ ] Implement per-item evaluation (inclusive, higher_is_worse).\n- [ ] Implement worst-state-wins aggregation.\n- [ ] Return a single aggregated state payload for rendering (green/yellow/red) and standard reason/debug metadata if supported.\n\n### Frontend\n- [ ] Implement/verify widget rendering uses the framework’s standard widget rendering + standard cover UI.\n- [ ] Ensure add/edit UI supports multiselect of items and threshold input fields.\n- [ ] Ensure direction is not user-editable (or is rejected server-side).\n\n### Testing\n- [ ] Unit tests for:\n  - [ ] inclusive comparisons at exact threshold values\n  - [ ] aggregation worst-state-wins across combinations\n- [ ] Integration/API tests for refresh handler:\n  - [ ] invalid config thresholds => INVALID_CONFIG cover\n  - [ ] empty selection => empty/no-items cover\n  - [ ] permission-lost items => permission/inaccessible cover\n  - [ ] unsupported item types => unsupported-items cover\n  - [ ] missing latest values => no-data cover\n- [ ] Persistence test:\n  - [ ] save → reload dashboard → verify config restored and refresh works\n- [ ] Manual verification: Add widget via dashboard picker, configure multiselect + thresholds, confirm traffic-light state transitions at inclusive boundaries.\n- [ ] Manual verification: Modify user permissions to remove access to one configured item, refresh, and confirm correct standard cover with no data leakage.\n- [ ] Manual verification: Configure widget with an unsupported numeric item type and confirm the standard unsupported-items cover.\n- [ ] Manual verification: Create a scenario with no latest value for one selected item (per framework definition) and confirm the standard no-data cover.\n\n## Verification\n- Automated verification (CI/tooling-dependent):\n  - Run unit/integration tests added for widget evaluation semantics and validation/cover mapping.\n  - Validate persistence serialization/deserialization with existing widget framework test harness.\n- Manual verification:\n  - Confirm UI widget picker registration and the add/edit/save/load/refresh flows behave as expected.\n  - Visually confirm the widget shows exactly one traffic-light state (not per-item) and matches worst-state-wins.\n  - Confirm inclusive boundary semantics by testing values exactly at thresholds.\n\n## Documentation Impact\n- [ ] Add/update widget documentation entry for the new widget (under existing dashboard widget documentation) including:\n  - supported item types (float and unsigned integer, or narrower subset enforced by the framework)\n  - fixed MVP semantics: `higher_is_worse`, inclusive, worst-state-wins\n  - explanation of aggregated behavior\n- [ ] If the framework enforces a narrower subset of numeric types, document that explicitly in implementation notes and/or widget docs.\n- [ ] If there is a widget registry/overview list, register the new widget id, config fields, and output semantics.\n\n## Suggested Diagram\n```puml\n@startuml\nleft to right direction\nskinparam ParticipantPadding 20\nskinparam BoxPadding 10\nskinparam ArrowColor #333\n\nactor User\nparticipant \"Dashboard widget framework\\n(widget refresh route)\" as Framework\nparticipant \"WorstStateNumericWidget (PHP)\" as Widget\ndatabase \"Items DB (latest numeric value)\" as ItemsDB\n\nUser -> Framework : Request widget refresh/view\nFramework -> Widget : refresh(config, userCtx)\n\nalt invalid widget config (parse/ordering/rules)\n  Widget -> Framework : Return standard cover\\nINVALID_CONFIG\nelse empty selection (no itemids)\n  Widget -> Framework : Return standard cover\\nEMPTY / NO_ITEMS\nelse resolve configured itemids\n  Widget -> Widget : Validate thresholds floats\\nEnsure red_threshold >= yellow_threshold\\n(upper is worse; inclusive)\n  Widget -> Widget : Resolve itemids accessible to user\n  alt permission loss/inaccessible items\n    Widget -> Framework : Return standard cover\\nPERMISSION / INACCESSIBLE\n  else supported numeric item types\n    Widget -> Widget : Filter/verify supported numeric types\\n(float, unsigned int subset)\n    alt unsupported items present\n      Widget -> Framework : Return standard cover\\nUNSUPPORTED_ITEMS\n    else fetch latest values per item\n      Widget -> ItemsDB : Get latest numeric value per itemid\n      alt no data / missing value\n        Widget -> Framework : Return standard cover\\nNO_DATA\n      else evaluate per-item MVP semantics\n        Widget -> Widget : For each item:\n        - red if value >= red_threshold\n        - else yellow if value >= yellow_threshold\n        - else green\n        - include reason metadata\n        Widget -> Widget : Aggregate worst-state-wins:\n        any red -> red\n        else any yellow -> yellow\n        else green\n      end\n      Widget -> Framework : Return traffic-light payload\\n(state=G/Y/R)\n    end\n  end\nend\n\nFramework -> User : Render widget UI\\n- traffic-light HTML\\n- or standard cover\n@enduml\n```",
    "rationale": "This refined ticket locks down the fixed MVP semantics (higher_is_worse, inclusive thresholds, worst-state-wins), clarifies validation and standard cover mappings for all required failure modes, and makes backend/frontend/testing boundaries explicit to ensure the widget integrates cleanly with the existing widget framework. It also preserves the decision-tree PlantUML diagram to reduce ambiguity in cover selection and refresh-time evaluation."
  }
}

  "title": "Implement aggregated numeric traffic-light widget (worst-state-wins, higher_is_worse)",
  "description": "## Goal\nAdd a new PHP dashboard widget under `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` that lets users select one or more **supported numeric items** and configure shared **inclusive** `yellow_threshold` and `red_threshold` values. On refresh, the widget evaluates each selected item’s latest numeric value using fixed MVP semantics (**higher_is_worse**, **worst-state-wins**, inclusive boundaries) and renders **one aggregated traffic-light** state (green/yellow/red).\n\n**MVP semantics (fixed):**\n- Aggregation mode: `worst-state-wins`\n  - any red => widget red\n  - else any yellow => widget yellow\n  - else all green => widget green\n- Threshold direction: `higher_is_worse`\n- Boundary behavior: inclusive comparisons\n- Per-item evaluation (inclusive, higher_is_worse):\n  - `value >= red_threshold` => per-item **red**\n  - else `value >= yellow_threshold` => per-item **yellow**\n  - else => per-item **green**\n\nSupported items (enforced by implementation notes/widget framework):\n- numeric items only; specifically **float** and **unsigned integer** (or the narrower subset enforced by the widget framework—**document explicitly in implementation notes**).\n\nWidget must integrate with the existing dashboard widget framework lifecycle (picker + add/edit/save/load + permission-aware render/refresh + persistence) and must use **standard widget covers** (no custom partial UI) for:\n- invalid configuration\n- empty selection\n- permission loss / inaccessible items\n- unsupported items\n- no-data conditions\n\n## Scope\nIn scope:\n1. Create one new widget type in the existing PHP UI widget framework under:\n   - `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/`\n2. Register it so it appears in the dashboard widget picker and participates in all standard widget flows:\n   - add/edit/save/load\n   - permission-aware render/refresh\n   - persistence/serialization\n3. Implement configuration fields:\n   - widget title/name (standard framework field)\n   - multiselect of **one or more items**\n   - shared `yellow_threshold` (numeric/float input)\n   - shared `red_threshold` (numeric/float input)\n   - `direction` is fixed to `higher_is_worse` (either hidden or validated server-side as invariant per framework conventions)\n4. Implement server-side validation during render/refresh (and optionally save-time if supported):\n   - threshold parsing as numeric (float)\n   - enforce ordering compatible with `higher_is_worse`:\n     - require `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold` (inclusive boundaries; equality allowed)\n   - ignore/deny any client-provided direction to keep invariant `higher_is_worse`\n5. Implement per-item evaluation and aggregation on refresh:\n   - resolve configured item ids with permission checks\n   - fetch each item’s latest numeric value\n   - map each to per-item state via inclusive comparisons\n   - aggregate via worst-state-wins\n\nOut of scope (for this single backlog item):\n- additional aggregation modes other than `worst-state-wins`\n- configurable `direction` or switching threshold direction semantics\n- custom non-standard widget UI components beyond the framework’s standard traffic-light/cover rendering\n\n## System Changes\nBackend (PHP, widget framework integration):\n- Add new widget backend under `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/`:\n  - manifest/registration entry so the widget is discoverable in picker\n  - server widget controller/view implementation consistent with existing widget framework conventions\n  - endpoints/handlers required by the widget framework for refresh and persistence\n- Configuration handling & persistence:\n  - store and reload title, selected item ids, `yellow_threshold`, `red_threshold`\n  - persist multiselect selection\n- Validation/invariants on refresh/render:\n  - parse thresholds as float\n  - validate `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`\n  - enforce `direction = higher_is_worse` as invariant (server-side)\n  - validate selected items are supported numeric types (float + unsigned integer, or narrower enforced subset; explicitly document)\n- Permission-aware item resolution:\n  - load configured items using existing permission-aware utilities\n  - inaccessible items must not leak values; selection may become empty post-filter\n- Data retrieval:\n  - fetch each resolved item’s latest numeric value using the framework’s established “latest data point” pattern\n  - robust numeric extraction/conversion with framework-consistent no-data handling\n- State computation:\n  - per-item:\n    - `value >= red_threshold` => red\n    - else if `value >= yellow_threshold` => yellow\n    - else green\n  - aggregation:\n    - any red => aggregated red\n    - else any yellow => aggregated yellow\n    - else green\n- Standard cover usage (no custom partial UI):\n  - invalid config\n  - empty selection (including after permission filter)\n  - permission loss/inaccessible items\n  - unsupported items\n  - no-data conditions\n\nFrontend/Assets (framework-compatible):\n- Implement widget frontend assets only as required by the framework:\n  - render aggregated traffic-light state using the framework’s standard HTML/CSS/state mechanism\n  - use the framework’s standard covers for error/empty/permission/no-data cases\n- Widget configuration UI:\n  - title/name uses standard widget field\n  - item multiselect picker uses framework standard picker component\n  - numeric input fields for `yellow_threshold` and `red_threshold`\n  - `direction` not user-editable (hide or enforce as invariant)\n\nSuggested PlantUML diagram is included in “Suggested Diagram”.\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n- The widget appears in the dashboard widget picker and can be added/configured/saved via the standard widget framework flows.\n- Selecting **1+ supported numeric items** and configuring thresholds results in exactly **one aggregated traffic-light** state on refresh.\n- Inclusive boundary behavior is correct:\n  - value exactly equal to `yellow_threshold` => yellow\n  - value exactly equal to `red_threshold` => red\n- Aggregation semantics are correct:\n  - any red among items => aggregated red\n  - else any yellow => aggregated yellow\n  - else green\n- Direction semantics are fixed to `higher_is_worse`:\n  - any attempt to override direction client-side is ignored/rejected server-side\n- The widget renders **standard framework covers** for:\n  - invalid configuration\n  - empty selection\n  - permission loss / inaccessible items\n  - unsupported items\n  - no-data conditions\n- Permissions are enforced:\n  - inaccessible items do not leak values\n  - resulting behavior matches framework cover conventions\n- Widget configuration persists and reloads correctly when dashboards are reopened.\n\n## Implementation Checklist\n### Backend\n- [ ] Create `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` with required widget backend entry points (controller/view, config handler, refresh handler) following existing framework patterns.\n- [ ] Register the widget in the widget manifest system so it appears in the picker.\n- [ ] Implement config schema handling:\n  - title/name (standard)\n  - multiselect item ids\n  - `yellow_threshold` and `red_threshold` parsing as float\n  - invariant enforcement: direction fixed to `higher_is_worse`\n- [ ] Implement validation:\n  - reject invalid/NaN thresholds\n  - enforce `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`\n  - enforce supported numeric item types (document exact allowed types in implementation notes)\n- [ ] Implement permission-aware item resolution:\n  - filter item ids by user permissions\n  - if resolved set is empty after filtering => standard cover per framework convention\n- [ ] Implement latest numeric value fetching per resolved item.\n- [ ] Implement per-item state mapping with inclusive comparisons.\n- [ ] Implement worst-state-wins aggregation.\n- [ ] Implement standard cover selection logic for:\n  - invalid config / empty selection / unsupported items / no-data / permission-filtered emptiness.\n- [ ] Ensure persistence roundtrip: save => reload => refresh computes same aggregated state.\n\n### Frontend\n- [ ] Add widget frontend assets under `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/assets/` if the framework requires it.\n- [ ] Ensure the widget renders aggregated state using the framework’s standard traffic-light state rendering (no custom partial UI).\n- [ ] Ensure the widget configuration form uses framework-standard controls:\n  - item multiselect\n  - numeric inputs for thresholds\n  - title/name field\n- [ ] Ensure `direction` is not editable (or is ignored/blocked server-side).\n- [ ] Wire refresh response handling to update only the aggregated traffic-light state.\n\n### Testing\n- [ ] Unit tests (PHP) for:\n  - threshold parsing and validation (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`)\n  - per-item inclusive mapping at exact boundaries\n  - worst-state-wins aggregation correctness\n- [ ] Integration/API tests for:\n  - refresh endpoint computing aggregated state from seeded item values\n  - behavior when permission filtering removes all selected items\n  - behavior when selected items include unsupported numeric types\n  - behavior when items have no latest value (no-data)\n- [ ] UI behavior tests (if repo has UI test harness):\n  - aggregated traffic-light updates on refresh\n  - correct CSS/state classes for green/yellow/red\n- [ ] Manual verification:\n  - Manual verification: Add the widget to a dashboard and configure 2–3 items with values around thresholds; verify inclusive boundaries.\n  - Manual verification: Set one item to meet red and ensure aggregated state becomes red regardless of other items.\n  - Manual verification: Change user permissions so selected items become inaccessible; verify the standard permission/no-data/empty cover behavior.\n  - Manual verification: Attempt to select unsupported item types (or simulate via backend); verify unsupported-item cover.\n\n## Verification\n- Automated verification (runner-friendly):\n  - Unit: deterministic per-item mapping and aggregation tests.\n  - Integration/API: seeded latest-value refresh responses and cover-selection codes/types per framework.\n- Manual verification:\n  - Manual verification: End-to-end dashboard flow (picker -> add -> configure -> save -> reopen -> refresh) confirms persistence and correct aggregated traffic-light visuals.\n  - Manual verification: Visual confirmation that the widget uses standard covers for invalid/empty/permission/no-data/unsupported states.\n\n## Documentation Impact\n- Add/extend widget documentation entry (if there is a widget catalog/docs page):\n  - widget id/name\n  - supported item types (float/unsigned integer; or narrower enforced subset)\n  - fixed semantics: higher_is_worse + inclusive thresholds + worst-state-wins\n  - config fields and invariants\n- Add a short developer note in the widget implementation notes describing:\n  - the exact supported numeric item type enforcement and where it is applied\n  - cover behavior mapping to framework conventions\n\n## Suggested Diagram\n```puml\n@startuml\nleft to right direction\nactor \"Dashboard\" as Dash\nparticipant \"WidgetView (PHP)\") as WidgetView\nparticipant \"Config validation (server)\" as Validate\nparticipant \"Item resolver + permissions\" as Resolve\nparticipant \"Numeric fetch (latest value)\" as Fetch\nparticipant \"Per-item state mapping\" as MapState\nparticipant \"Aggregation (worst-state-wins)\" as Aggregate\nparticipant \"Render (traffic-light / cover)\" as Render\n\nDash -> WidgetView : refresh(widget_id, persisted_config)\nWidgetView -> Validate : validate thresholds + invariants\nValidate : enforce direction = higher_is_worse\nValidate : inclusive bounds\nValidate : require red_threshold >= yellow_threshold\nValidate : validate selected items are supported numeric\n\nalt invalid config/empty selection/unsupported items/no-data\n  WidgetView -> Render : standard cover (invalid/empty/unsupported/no-data)\nelse valid\n  WidgetView -> Resolve : load configured item ids with permission checks\n  Resolve : filter inaccessible items\n\n  alt after permission filter selection empty\n    WidgetView -> Render : standard cover (no-data/inaccessible)\n  else\n    WidgetView -> Fetch : fetch latest numeric value per item\n    Fetch -> MapState : values\n    MapState : per item:\n    MapState : if value >= red_threshold => red\n    MapState : else if value >= yellow_threshold => yellow\n    MapState : else => green\n\n    MapState -> Aggregate : per-item states\n    Aggregate : worst-state-wins\n    Aggregate : any red => red\n    Aggregate : else any yellow => yellow\n    Aggregate : else => green\n\n    Aggregate -> WidgetView : aggregated_state (green/yellow/red)\n    WidgetView -> Render : render traffic-light container/state\n  end\nend\n@enduml\n```",

(invalid/empty/unsupported/no-data)\\nelse valid\\n  WidgetView -> Resolve : load configured item ids with permission checks\\n  Resolve : filter inaccessible items\\n\\n  alt after permission filter selection empty\\n    WidgetView -> Render : standard cover (no-data/inaccessible)\\n  else\\n    WidgetView -> Fetch : fetch latest numeric value per item\\n    Fetch -> MapState : values\\n    MapState : per item:\\n    MapState : if value >= red_threshold => red\\n    MapState : else if value >= yellow_threshold => yellow\\n    MapState : else => green\\n\\n    MapState -> Aggregate : per-item states\\n    Aggregate : worst-state-wins\\n    Aggregate : any red => red\\n    Aggregate : else any yellow => yellow\\n    Aggregate : else => green\\n\\n    Aggregate -> WidgetView : aggregated_state (green/yellow/red)\\n    WidgetView -> Render : render traffic-light container/state\\n  end\\nend\\n@enduml\\n```\",\n\ncodex\n```json\n{\n  \"answer\": \"no\",\n  \"reason\": \"The
